([SpikeboyIL/claude-code-discipline-pack](https://github.com/SpikeboyIL/claude-code-discipline-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Adversarially challenge a named high-risk product or technical decision with a fresh host-native reviewer when explicitly requested. Reconcile evidence, assumptions, failure modes, reversibility, and safe experiments. ([swimmwatch/engineer-agent-workflows](https://github.com/swimmwatch/engineer-agent-workflows/tree/HEAD/plugins/codex/engineer-agent-workflows/skills/doubt-driven-development) · 4 copies)
- **doubt-driven-development**: 怀疑驱动开发（DDD）——每个非平凡决策在定稿前，由新上下文审查者以'反驳'为偏见进行审查。核心五步：CLAIM→EXTRACT→DOUBT→RECONCILE→STOP。触发词：怀疑、质疑、确认一下这个决策、审查设计、决策审查、这个方案对吗、我们先质疑一下、doubt this、challenge this decision。适用于编码过程中的非平凡决策，与 adversarial-review 互补（后者是成品 PR 事后审查）。 ([tataCrayon/ai-coding-workflow](https://github.com/tataCrayon/ai-coding-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development) · 2 copies)
- **doubt-driven-development**: Subjects every non-trivial decision to a fresh-context adversarial review before it stands. Use when correctness matters more than speed, when working in unfamiliar code, when stakes are high (production, security-sensitive logic, irreversible operations), or any time a confident output would be cheaper to verify now than to debug later. Consumed by: devils-advocate agent (Mode A — explicitly operationalises this technique at team level). Also invokable standalone via /challenge. ([tejaskatariya/sdlc-playground](https://github.com/tejaskatariya/sdlc-playground/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Adversarial fresh-context review of every non-trivial decision in-flight - CLAIM -&gt; EXTRACT -&gt; DOUBT -&gt; RECONCILE -&gt; STOP, with optional user-authorized cross-model escalation. Use when stakes are high (production, security, irreversible), working in unfamiliar code, or a confident output is cheaper to verify now than to debug later. ([th-yoo/gauntlet-loop](https://github.com/th-yoo/gauntlet-loop/tree/HEAD/oracle/fixtures/sampled/addyosmani__agent-skills/OUTPUT/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Adversarial review for a non-trivial implementation decision before it stands. Use when the user asks to "doubt" or stress-test a decision, when correctness matters more than speed, or when the choice crosses module boundaries, asserts something hard to verify, or is hard to reverse. ([toderian/project_template](https://github.com/toderian/project_template/tree/HEAD/plugins/agents-extras/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: 在每次非平凡决策"站住"前，把它交给 fresh-context 对抗式审查。在你想在动手前对每个假设交叉质询、为隐性失败模式压力测试一份规划、正确性的权重比速度更高、在不熟悉的代码里工作、风险高（生产认证、安全敏感逻辑、高风险迁移、不可逆操作）、或者一个自信的输出在现在验证比将来 debug 更便宜时使用。 ([TOKEN-DL/agent-skills-zh](https://github.com/TOKEN-DL/agent-skills-zh/tree/HEAD/skills/chinese-doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Đưa mọi quyết định không tầm thường qua một đợt đánh giá đối kháng với ngữ cảnh mới (fresh-context adversarial review) trước khi chốt. Sử dụng khi tính chính xác quan trọng hơn tốc độ, khi làm việc trong mã nguồn không quen thuộc, khi rủi ro cao (môi trường production, logic nhạy cảm về bảo mật, các thao tác không thể đảo ngược), hoặc bất cứ khi nào mà việc xác minh một kết quả tự tin ở hiện tại sẽ rẻ hơn so với việc gỡ lỗi (debug) sau này. ([tuanfung/pqt-agent-skills](https://github.com/tuanfung/pqt-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Use when stakes are high â€” adversarial fresh-context review of every non-trivial decision. ([viasrijan/crazy-skills](https://github.com/viasrijan/crazy-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/extended/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Stress-test an uncertain design or correctness claim while the work is still in flight, using a bounded fresh-context reviewer biased to disprove it, when requested or justified by concrete risk. Not a finished-PR review (use code-review); use authorized available reviewers and avoid repeated approvals for an already-chosen workflow. ([w0rxbend/dot-agents](https://github.com/w0rxbend/dot-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: Cross-examines a non-trivial decision with a fresh-context reviewer briefed to disprove it, before it stands. Use when correctness beats speed — unfamiliar code, security-sensitive logic, irreversible operations, or an assertion the compiler cannot check like thread safety, idempotence, or ordering. ([yenchang265-afk/agentic-workflow](https://github.com/yenchang265-afk/agentic-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: 对每一个非平凡的决策在全新上下文中进行对抗性审查，然后才予通过。当正确性比速度更重要时，当在不熟悉的代码中工作时，当风险较高时（生产环境、安全敏感逻辑、不可逆操作），或者当现在验证比事后调试更便宜时，使用此技能。 ([zhliner/aiTips](https://github.com/zhliner/aiTips/tree/HEAD/addyosmani/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development**: 在每个非平凡决策生效之前，对其进行全新上下文的对抗性审查。当正确性比速度更重要时，当在不熟悉的代码中工作时，当风险较高时（生产环境、安全敏感逻辑、不可逆操作），或者当现在验证比事后调试更便宜时，使用此技能。 ([zhliner/aiTips](https://github.com/zhliner/aiTips/tree/HEAD/addyosmani/skills_cn/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-development（怀疑驱动开发）**: 在每个非简单决策成立之前，让它接受新鲜上下文的对抗性审查。在正确性比速度更重要时、在不熟悉的代码中工作时、在高风险时（生产环境、安全敏感逻辑、不可逆操作）、或任何自信的输出现在验证比以后调试更便宜时使用。 ([guigu856/forge](https://github.com/guigu856/forge/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doubt-driven-development))
- **doubt-driven-review**: In-flight adversarial check on a non-trivial decision BEFORE it stands — distinct from post-hoc review of a finished diff. Use on "stress-test this decision", "are we sure about this", "verify before commit", "poke holes in this", when working in unfamiliar code, or before an irreversible step (migration, prod deploy, public API). Runs earlier than code-review, while course-correction is cheap. ([BlackBeltTechnology/pi-agent-dashboard](https://github.com/BlackBeltTechnology/pi-agent-dashboard/tree/HEAD/packages/eng-disciplines/.pi/skills/doubt-driven-review) · ★353)
- **doubt-driven-review**: Runs an adversarial review gate for non-trivial or high-stakes decisions before they stand. Use when Codex is about to claim something is safe, correct, reversible, production-ready, scalable, idempotent, secure, or compliant; when work touches production, auth, permissions, secrets, billing, public APIs, migrations, irreversible cleanup, customer data, external agent CLIs, or unfamiliar code with meaningful blast radius; or when a confident answer would be cheaper to challenge now than to debug later. ([pimenov/codex-first-skills-pack](https://github.com/pimenov/codex-first-skills-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/doubt-driven-review) · ★29)
- **doubt-driven-review**: Revisa afirmacoes e decisoes com duvida sistematica e evidencias. - **doubt-driven-review**: Adversarial fresh-context review for non-trivial decisions before they stand. - **doubt-driven-zh**: 质疑驱动开发、对非平凡决策做冷上下文的对抗性复审、在提交前找漏洞。当用户说「帮我挑刺」「这段代码/方案哪里会出问题」「上线前多一双眼睛」「我不太确定这个改动安全」「让另一个模型审一下」「高风险迁移先审」，或改动涉及分支逻辑、跨模块边界、编译器验不了的属性（线程安全、幂等、顺序）、不可逆操作时使用。五步：CLAIM 写清主张与为何重要 → EXTRACT 剥出最小可审单元（产物 + 契约，不带你的推理）→ DOUBT 用"找问题、不要认可"的对抗提示交给冷上下文审稿者（只给产物与契约，不给主张；交互式必须提供跨模型二审的选项）→ RECONCILE 逐条按 契约误读/有效可行动/有效权衡/噪音 分类 → STOP 只剩琐碎、3 轮或用户拍板即停。改编自 addyosmani/agent-skills 的 doubt-driven-development（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/doubt-driven-zh) · ★4)
- **doubt-escalation**: In-flight escalation (Doubt model) for the dowiz/DeliveryOS harness. When an OBSERVABLE signal fires mid-task (a loop of N failed attempts, a red-line/irreversible edit, ≥2 surviving interpretations, evidence conflict, novelty, or a result that contradicts a stated expectation), climb the cheapest-sufficient escalation rung within budget K — self-divergence → specialist/research subagent → stronger model → council → human — then RESUME the main task. Friction, not a hard stop, except irreversible/red-line which gate to a human. Routine reversible low-uncertainty work never escalates. ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-escalation) · ★2)
- **doubt-escalation**: How any agent resolves doubts without guessing: triage (resolve, assume or escalate), a doubt taxonomy with a routing table to quick-researcher, deep-researcher, fact-checker, tester, quality-control, code-quality-analyst, adversarial-verifier, file-summarizer or the caller, the doubt brief template, cost limits, integrating answers and recording unresolved doubts. Preloaded into every agent. ([rustcircle/c101](https://github.com/rustcircle/c101/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/doubt-escalation))
- **doubt-gate**: Cross-examine a non-trivial decision in-flight — before it stands — with a fresh-context reviewer biased to disprove. The in-flight complement to adversarial-verify (which refutes finished output). Use for branching logic, boundary crossings, properties the compiler can't check (thread-safety, idempotence, ordering, invariants), or irreversible blast radius (deploy, migration, public API). - **doubt-it**: Adversarially check a claim before acting on it, using a fresh-context subagent that tries to prove it wrong. - **doubt-pass**: Adversarial doubt cycle on a plan or design before any code exists — distinct from post-hoc code review. - **doubt-pass**: Fresh-context adversarial judge of a decision-ledger candidate. Reads only the intent, surface map, and bare decisions — never the author's rationale — and reports what is wrong, missing, or unconsidered. - **doubt-review**: Load before a non-trivial claim of yours stands that no compiler or test will check — "this is safe", "this is idempotent", "this cannot race", "this is the root cause", "this migration is backwards compatible", "this scales" — and before an irreversible step built on such a claim. - **doudou-dev**: 跨平台（Windows、macOS、Linux）管理 doudou-ai-coding 开发与构建流程。包含两大核心模式：(1) 开发调试模式（通过“调试/开发/启动/运行/本地跑起来/dev”等触发），启动 doudou-eggjs (npm run dev) 与 doudou-vue3 (pnpm dev)，提供端口与环境探测、依赖自动安装、并发日志与优雅退出；(2) 生产打包模式（通过“打包/构建/编译产物/生产打包/build/build:prod”等触发），执行 doudou-eggjs (npm run build，即 rec build -o dist) 与 doudou-vue3 (pnpm run build:prod，即 vite build) 生成发布产物。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-dev) · ★209 · 3 copies)
- **doudou-doc**: 把办公文档转成 Markdown（Office / 电子书 / PDF → Markdown）。支持 Word（doc/docx/odt/rtf）、PPT（ppt/pptx/odp）、表格（xlsx/ods/csv）、以及 pdf 和 epub。当用户提供本地文档文件、文档目录或远程文档 URL，并希望提取正文、转成 Markdown、喂给大模型阅读总结、抽取表格数据、或把一批合同/报表/论文批量转文本时使用此技能。注意本技能不做 OCR：扫描件与纯图片 PDF 没有文字层，那类输入走 PaddleOCR。用户没有指定文件或目录时，默认转换项目根目录下 `resources/data/` 里的文档。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-doc) · ★209 · 2 copies)
- **doudou-editorial-cover**: 为“摆烂的逗逗”制作或修改米白纸感、深石油蓝与铜赭重点色的技术档案风视频封面。用于用户要求复现已认可的多层级配色技术档案封面、按此风格制作新主题或调整封面层级；锁定视觉语言与边距，按主题灵活排版。 ([doudou864/doudou-editorial-cover](https://github.com/doudou864/doudou-editorial-cover/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **doudou-ocr**: 从图片和 PDF 里把文字提取成文本（OCR / 文字识别）。支持截图取字、扫描件与拍照文档识别、发票与票据取数、PDF 转文本、以及日英法德等 10 种语言的识别。当用户提供图片（png/jpg/bmp/gif/tiff/webp）或 PDF，并希望读出上面的文字、抄下截图里的内容、把扫描件或拍的照片变成可编辑文本、提取表单/发票/名片信息、或者让模型「看懂」一张图上写了什么时使用此技能。用户没有指定文件或目录时，默认识别项目根目录下 `resources/data/` 里的图片与 PDF。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-ocr) · ★209 · 2 copies)
- **doudou-product**: 将用户提供的需求资料或参考项目链接转化为面向产品、设计、研发和测试的完整产品设计交付物：产品需求文档（PRD）、设计交付说明、数据库表结构 SQL、研发实现说明和测试验收用例；当用户提供网站、Web 应用、后台系统、移动端 H5 或原型的参考链接时，使用 chrome-devtools-mcp 深入浏览和交互分析，并输出参考项目分析.md。用户提到“产品设计”、需求梳理、PRD、业务流程、功能流程、数据表设计、根据需求设计数据库、参考项目、竞品链接、分析这个网站/系统/页面或希望复刻某个产品功能时，主动使用此技能。开始前先识别资料来源（知识库、本地目录、参考链接或两两结合），阅读/分析资料后确认数据库类型及表名、字段名命名规范，再进行设计并输出完整交付物。用户未指定资料位置也未提供参考项目链接时，默认通过 doudou-wiki 技能从项目知识库（`kb/`）获取需求资料。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-product) · ★209 · 3 copies)

- **doudou-wiki**: LLM 驱动的个人知识库（LLM Wiki）。把素材编译成一份持续复利的结构化 wiki： 收录素材、基于知识库回答问题、健康检查、重置删除。素材不限文本 —— 音视频、Office 文档、 PDF、扫描件、图片都能收，分别交给 doudou-stt / doudou-doc / doudou-ocr 技能预处理成文本再收录。 TRIGGER when: 用户提到「收录」「加到知识库」「归档这个」「add」，或让「把这段录音/这份合同/ 这张截图整理进知识库」，或提出知识库中可能有答案的问题（如「XX 是什么」「对比 XX 和 YY」 「整理一下 XX 的信息」），或要求「检查知识库」「体检」，或要求「清空」「删除」「重置」知识库， 或给出对知识库操作方式的反馈/偏好。 DO NOT TRIGGER when: 问题与知识库无关（纯代码任务、文件操作、通用对话），或用户只想单纯地 转个字幕/转个文档而不入库（那种直接用 doudou-stt / doudou-doc / doudou-ocr）， 或用户明确说不需要查知识库。 ([undsky/doudou-ai-coding](https://github.com/undsky/doudou-ai-coding/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/doudou-wiki) · ★209 · 3 copies)

- **douyin-caption**: 从抖音分享链接提取视频简介/图文长文，优先使用文中标题，否则总结标题，AI 分段排版后追加看法、评价与总结，保存为 Markdown。当用户提到「抖音文案」「提取简介」「抖音长文」「保存成 md」「分段」「排版」「看法」「评价」「总结」「v.douyin.com 文案」时使用。这不是语音转写，也不下载视频。 ([fengfe1125/douyin-caption](https://github.com/fengfe1125/douyin-caption/tree/HEAD/skills/douyin-caption))
- **douyin-cdp-draft**: 用「用户本机的 Google Chrome（专用自动化 profile + CDP 9222）」把一集成片视频 + 标题/简介(含#话题)/封面填进抖音创作者中心（creator.douyin.com）的发布表单，填完停住，由用户人工点击「发布」完成提交（本 skill 永不代点）。 适用场景：收到「发布 &lt;ep&gt; 到抖音」工单后，基于 data/projects/&lt;slug&gt;/publish/&lt;platform&gt;/package/&lt;ep&gt;/ 的发布包（seo/metadata 取 publish/seo.json、publish/metadata.json 的本集条目）自动完成视频上传与 meta 信息填写、登录态检查、CDP 截图确认。 ([AgenticsWorld/VideoAgents](https://github.com/AgenticsWorld/VideoAgents/tree/HEAD/agents/12-publishing/publisher/skills/skill-douyin-cdp-draft) · ★1)
- **douyin-chaoxi**: 抖音公开数据智能获取工具。支持抖音关键词搜索、抖人作品抓取、获取作品评论、实时热榜跟踪，适用于短视频营销、竞品分析、舆情分析和热点监控，助力爆款内容策划与流量追踪。 ([engheng-art/douyin-chaoxi](https://github.com/engheng-art/douyin-chaoxi/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
